  • Moveworks: Senior Software Engineer, Core Infrastructure，重点是 agentic AI platform 的 foundational services、reliability、resilience、frameworks，让产品快速 scale，让 engineering team 更快构建 customer-facing features。并且 context 提到从 350+ customers 到 ServiceNow 8400+ enterprises 的更大 scale。

你要做的不是把同一份故事讲两遍，而是同一个背景换两种 framing。

1. 对 xAI Data Systems，应该怎么定位自己？#

定位：

I’m interested in the data systems layer behind model training: reliable ingestion, dataset versioning, orchestration, quality checks, lineage, internal tools, and feedback loops that let model teams train faster without losing reproducibility or trust in the data.

中文解释：

这个岗位不是纯 data engineering，也不是纯 ML modeling。它更像 training data platform：把 raw data 变成可发现、可清洗、可版本化、可调度、可复现、可审计、可用于 training/eval 的 dataset。你要强调你理解 model training 的数据生命周期。

2. xAI 方向最该展示的 8 个能力信号#

  1. Data lineage

每个 training run 能追溯用了哪些 dataset、filter、dedup、sampling、version。

  1. Dataset versioning

数据不是不断覆盖的 blob，而是 versioned artifact，支持 rollback、comparison、reproducibility。

  1. Quality gates
  • corrupt sample、duplicate、toxicity、PII、license、format error、low-quality synthetic data 都要有 checks。
  1. Orchestration

pipeline DAG、retry、backfill、incremental update、idempotency、DLQ、checkpoint。

  1. Training throughput

数据系统最终要服务 GPU training，避免 dataloader、object storage、decode、shuffle 成瓶颈。

  1. Internal tooling
  • model/research team 需要 dataset browser、job status、quality dashboard、sample inspection、run comparison。
  1. Evaluation feedback loop

eval failure -> find bad data slice -> fix filter/sampling -> rerun training/eval。

  1. Operational maturity

monitoring、alerting、SLO、cost attribution、access control。

3. xAI recruiter screen 可以怎么讲？#

30 秒版本：

This sounds very aligned with the kind of infrastructure I like: systems that make model training faster and more reliable. I’d frame the work as building the data operating system for training - ingestion, dataset versioning, orchestration, quality checks, lineage, and internal tooling. The part I find interesting is making data iteration fast for researchers while still preserving reproducibility, observability, and trust in the training data.

如果对方问 “你为什么适合 data systems for model training？”

I can bring backend and infra thinking to ML workflows. For training systems, the hard problems are not just moving data, but making data discoverable, versioned, deduplicated, quality-checked, privacy/license-aware, and efficiently consumable by GPU training jobs. I also care about the internal tools that make these systems usable by model teams.

4. xAI system design 题：设计 model training data platform#

Requirements：

ingestion raw data。 · clean/filter/dedup。 · dataset versioning。 · orchestration。 · sample inspection。 · training run reproducibility。 · monitoring/cost。 · internal UI。

Architecture：

Ingestion Service · batch/stream/source connectors，schema detection，checksum。

Raw Data Lake · immutable raw data，partitioned by source/time/license/modality。

Processing Pipeline · parsing、normalization、dedup、quality scoring、PII/license filtering。

Metadata Catalog · dataset、sample counts、quality stats、source、license、owner、version。

Dataset Builder · declarative recipe：sources + filters + sampling + transforms。

Versioned Dataset Store · manifest-based dataset version，content-addressed shards。

Orchestrator · DAG execution、retry、backfill、incremental build、idempotency。

Training Interface · dataloader-friendly shards、local cache、streaming、shuffle。

Internal UI · dataset browser、sample viewer、pipeline status、quality dashboard、run comparison。

Lineage Store · training_run_id -> dataset_version -> recipe -> source snapshots -> code version。

Deep pain points：

Data pipeline 成功不代表数据好；需要 quality distribution。 · Dedup 很难：exact duplicate、near duplicate、semantic duplicate。 · Incremental update 和 reproducibility 有冲突。 · Model team 想快速试数据，infra 要保证安全和可追溯。 · GPU 训练很贵，不能让 dataloader 或 decode 成瓶颈。 · Synthetic data 可能提高能力，也可能引入模式坍塌或 contamination。

Metrics：

  • dataset build latency。
  • sample drop/reject rate by reason。
  • duplicate rate。
  • training dataloader throughput。
  • GPU input stall time。
  • dataset reproducibility。
  • pipeline failure recovery time。
  • time from data request to training-ready dataset。
  • eval lift by data slice。

Standout sentence：

For model training, data infrastructure should be measured not only by pipeline success, but by how quickly and reliably model teams can turn data changes into reproducible training and eval outcomes.

5. xAI 项目故事：Training Data Quality and Lineage Platform#

一句话：

I built a training data quality and lineage platform where every dataset version is generated from a declarative recipe, quality-checked, sample-inspectable, and linked to downstream training/eval results.

Story structure：

  • Problem：model quality regressions were hard to debug because nobody knew whether they came from code, model config, data mixture, filtering, or eval set changes。
  • Architecture：dataset recipe、processing DAG、quality metrics、dataset manifest、lineage table、sample viewer。
  • Workflow：researcher proposes dataset mixture -> pipeline builds version -> quality dashboard highlights anomalies -> training run records dataset version -> eval regression can trace back to changed slices。
  • Pain point：research wants speed, platform wants reproducibility。
  • Tradeoff：allow fast experimental datasets but mark them experimental; production training datasets require quality gates。
  • Impact：faster data iteration, fewer unreproducible training runs, better debugging of model regressions。

  • “Is the work more around batch pipelines, interactive tooling, or training-time data serving?”
  • “What does success look like for this team: faster data iteration, training throughput, data quality, reproducibility, or researcher productivity?”
  • “How do you handle synthetic data, contamination, and eval holdout protection?”

10. Moveworks system design 题：设计 enterprise agent execution platform#

Requirements：

agent receives user request。 · retrieves enterprise context。 · calls tools。 · respects permissions。 · handles retries/failures。 · logs audit。 · scales across many enterprises。 · supports product teams building new workflows。

Architecture：

Agent Gateway · auth、tenant resolution、request normalization、rate limit。

Context Service · retrieval from knowledge base, tickets, employee systems, permissions。

Planner · generate plan and tool calls。

Tool Registry · schema、owner、SLO、risk level、permission requirement、idempotency。

Policy Engine · tenant/user/action/resource checks。

Execution Engine · step execution、timeouts、retry、circuit breaker、compensation。

Workflow State Store · durable task state、resume、cancel、status。

Human Approval Service · required for high-risk actions。

Audit Log · immutable plan/tool/action records。

Observability · trace by request_id/task_id/tool_call_id/customer_id。

Developer Framework · SDK for teams to add new tools/workflows safely。

Pain points：

LLM may produce invalid tool calls。 · Tools have different latency/reliability。 · Enterprise permissions are complex。 · Agent task may partially fail。 · Some actions need approval。 · Multi-tenant scale means noisy neighbor risk。 · Product teams need flexibility without bypassing platform safety。

Metrics：

  • task success rate。
  • tool call success/latency。
  • retry rate。
  • approval rate。
  • policy block rate。
  • agent escalation rate。
  • customer-impacting incident count。
  • workflow authoring time for product teams。
  • SLO compliance by tenant。

Standout sentence：

In agentic AI infrastructure, the core design principle is separating reasoning from authority: the model can propose actions, but execution must go through deterministic policy, permissions, idempotency, audit, and rollback.

11. Moveworks 项目故事：Agentic Workflow Infrastructure#

一句话：

I built an agentic workflow infrastructure layer that lets teams add new enterprise workflows through tool contracts, policy checks, durable execution, observability, and approval gates.

Story structure：

  • Problem：teams were building one-off agent tools with inconsistent auth、retry、logging、approval。
  • Architecture：tool registry、execution engine、policy service、workflow state、audit log、observability SDK。
  • Workflow：feature team registers tool schema and risk level -> platform enforces permissions/retries/timeouts -> user request executes as durable workflow -> high-risk step waits for approval -> every step traceable。
  • Pain point：need developer velocity without unsafe shortcuts。
  • Tradeoff：framework constraints vs product flexibility。
  • Impact：new workflows faster to build, fewer reliability/security bugs, consistent customer trust。

17. xAI Deep Dive：Training Data Platform 该讲到什么深度？#

如果 xAI 面试深入，你要从 “ETL pipeline” 升级到 “model training data control plane + data plane”。

Control plane：

Dataset Catalog · dataset name、owner、source、license、modality、version、quality score、usage history。

  • Recipe Registry
  • declarative recipe：sources、filters、dedup policy、sampling weights、transforms、tokenization/packing config。
  • Lineage Store
  • dataset_version -> recipe_version -> source_snapshot -> pipeline_code_version -> training_run -> eval_result。
  • Policy and Access
  • who can use which source、PII/license restrictions、eval holdout protection、contamination guard。

Experiment Interface · researcher 可以 compare dataset versions、inspect samples、launch build、view quality diffs。

Data plane：

Raw Lake · immutable raw data，按 source/date/modality/license partition。

Processing Workers · parse、normalize、filter、dedup、quality score、PII redact、format conversion。

Dataset Manifest Builder · 生成 manifest，指向 content-addressed shards，而不是复制所有数据。

Training Data Serving · sharded files、streaming dataset、shuffle buffer、local cache、dataloader integration。

Quality Metrics Pipeline · sample-level and aggregate quality stats。

关键设计点：

Dataset version 应该是 manifest + recipe + source snapshot，不是一个会变的目录。 · Pipeline 必须 idempotent：同一个 recipe/source snapshot 重跑应得到一致结果。 · Incremental build 要能复用旧 shards，但 lineage 必须能说明哪些 shard 是新生成的。 · Training job 要记录 dataset version，否则 eval regression 无法归因。 · Internal UI 很关键，因为 researcher 需要看 sample，不只是看 aggregate metrics。

18. xAI Deep Dive：Dataset quality taxonomy#

数据质量不要只说 “clean data”。可以按 taxonomy 讲：

Format quality · parse error、encoding issue、corrupted media、bad JSON、truncated file。

Content quality · low information、spam、template junk、watermark-heavy、boilerplate。

Alignment quality · image-caption mismatch、video-caption mismatch、question-answer mismatch。

Safety quality · toxic、PII、secret、malware、unsafe instructions。

Legal quality · license、copyright、source restrictions、commercial usage。

Diversity quality · language/domain/topic/modality imbalance。

Duplication quality · exact dup、near dup、semantic dup、train/eval contamination。

Training utility · does this slice improve eval or only increase tokens?

Standout answer：

I would avoid treating data quality as a single score. I’d track a quality vector, because the action is different: corrupted samples need removal, low diversity needs resampling, contamination needs hard blocking, and alignment issues may need better labeling or model-based filtering.

19. xAI Deep Dive：Orchestration failure modes#

训练数据 pipeline 失败不只是 job failed。常见 failure modes：

  • partial success 但 manifest 已发布。
  • retry 导致 duplicate shard。
  • backfill 覆盖新版本。
  • source schema drift silently drops fields。
  • dedup index stale。
  • quality filter bug removes a useful domain。
  • PII detector false negative。
  • eval holdout accidentally included in training。
  • dataloader throughput drops after new compression format。

防御：

publish dataset version only after atomic validation。 · manifest write once，immutable。 · pipeline stage output content-addressed。 · schema contract tests。 · canary data build。 · quality diff before publish。 · holdout contamination check。 · training dry-run throughput check。

面试加分点：

I would treat dataset publication like a deployment. It needs validation, diff, approval policy for high-risk sources, rollback, and observability.

20. xAI Deep Dive：Internal tooling 该怎么讲？#

这个岗位提到 internal tooling 和 some UI，这是很好的差异化点。你可以讲：

Researcher UI should support：

  • dataset search and discovery。
  • recipe editor。
  • sample inspection。
  • before/after filter preview。
  • quality distribution。
  • duplicate clusters。
  • source/license view。
  • pipeline DAG status。
  • failed sample viewer。
  • dataset version diff。
  • training run linkage。
  • eval regression linkage。

为什么 UI 重要：

Model team 不应该靠 SQL 和 log grep 理解数据。 · Good UI 可以减少 platform team 的 ad-hoc support。 · Sample inspection 能发现 aggregate metrics 看不到的问题。

Standout line：

For training data systems, internal tooling is not cosmetic. It is how researchers build trust in the data and iterate without constantly asking infra engineers for custom queries.

21. xAI Deep Dive：Practical coding / design 可能怎么考？#

可能题型：

设计 dataset manifest format。 · 写一个 idempotent pipeline stage。 · 设计 retry + DLQ。 · 设计 dedup job。 · 设计 sample viewer backend API。 · 设计 training run lineage schema。 · Debug pipeline latency。 · Debug dataset version mismatch。

准备要点：

数据结构：manifest、shard、sample_id、content_hash、source_id、metadata。 · API：create dataset recipe、build dataset、get build status、list samples、compare versions。 · SQL/schema：dataset_versions、recipes、samples、shards、training_runs、eval_results。 · Distributed systems：idempotency key、atomic publish、checkpoint、backfill、retry。

Example schema：

text
dataset_version(
  dataset_id,
  version_id,
  recipe_hash,
  source_snapshot_ids,
  manifest_uri,
  created_by,
  created_at,
  quality_summary,
  status
)

training_run(
  run_id,
  model_config_hash,
  dataset_version_id,
  code_version,
  started_at,
  metrics_uri
)

22. Moveworks Deep Dive：Core infra 该讲到什么深度？#

Moveworks context 里关键词是 Core Infrastructure、agentic AI platform、foundational services、frameworks、reliable/resilient、customer-facing features rapidly、350+ customers -> ServiceNow 8400+ enterprises。

你要把自己从 “会做 backend” 提升到：

I build platform primitives that make enterprise agentic workflows safe, reliable, observable, and fast for product teams to extend.

Core infra platform layers：

Identity / Tenant Layer · tenant config、user identity、RBAC、resource permissions。

Agent Runtime Layer · planner、tool calling、workflow state、retry、timeout、approval。

Tool Platform · tool registry、schema validation、SLO、risk level、owner、version。

Knowledge / Context Layer · retrieval connectors、permission-filtered context、freshness、cache。

Policy Layer · action/resource/user/tenant decision。

Reliability Layer · circuit breaker、bulkhead、rate limit、degraded mode、DLQ。

Observability Layer · request/task/tool trace、customer impact、SLO、audit。

Developer Platform · SDK、templates、golden path、local test harness、simulator。

23. Moveworks Deep Dive：Agent workflow state machine#

Agentic workflow 不能只是一串 function calls。应该是 durable state machine：

text
received
  -> authenticated
  -> context_retrieved
  -> plan_generated
  -> plan_validated
  -> tool_execution_pending
  -> tool_executed
  -> approval_required?
  -> response_generated
  -> completed / failed / escalated / canceled

每一步要记录：

  • input/output。
  • model version。
  • tool version。
  • policy decision。
  • latency。
  • retry count。
  • error code。
  • customer/tenant。
  • audit metadata。

为什么要 durable：

enterprise workflows 可能跨系统、跨时间。 · tool failure 后要 resume。 · approval 可能几小时后才发生。 · partial execution 需要 compensation。 · incident debug 需要 replay timeline。

24. Moveworks Deep Dive：Tool registry 设计#

Tool registry 不是简单 function list。每个 tool 要有 production contract：

  • name
  • owner
  • schema
  • version
  • timeout
  • SLO
  • risk_level
  • required_permissions
  • idempotency_key
  • side_effect_type
  • approval_required
  • rate_limit
  • retry_policy
  • rollback/compensation
  • audit_fields
  • test fixtures

Example：

text
tool: create_servicenow_ticket
risk_level: medium
side_effect_type: creates_external_record
required_permissions: ticket:create
approval_required: false
idempotency_key: tenant_id + requester_id + request_hash
timeout: 5s
retry_policy: retry_on_5xx, no_retry_on_4xx
audit_fields: requester, ticket_type, target_system, payload_hash

Standout line：

A tool registry is how an agent platform turns arbitrary integrations into governed, observable, testable capabilities.

25. Moveworks Deep Dive：Reliability patterns for agentic AI#

Agentic systems 的 reliability 比普通 request/response 更难，因为：

LLM output 不稳定。 · tools 可能慢或失败。 · permissions 可能复杂。 · action 可能有 side effect。 · 用户期待自然语言体验，但系统背后是 distributed workflow。

Patterns：

Schema validation · invalid tool call 不执行。

Policy before execution · permission/resource check 先于 tool call。

Idempotency · retry 不创建重复 ticket / duplicate action。

Timeout and budget · 整个 task 有 budget，每个 tool 有 timeout。

Circuit breaker · 下游系统异常时 stop calling, fallback/escalate。

Bulkhead · 一个 customer/tool 不能拖垮全平台。

Graceful degradation · tool down 时给 explanation、create draft、escalate to human。

Compensation · partial side effect 要有 rollback 或 corrective action。

Audit · enterprise trust 必须能解释 agent 做了什么。

26. Moveworks Deep Dive：Scale jump 的设计压力#

Context 提到从 350+ customers 到 8400+ enterprises potential。这个 scale jump 不是简单 QPS 乘法，还包括：

  • tenant config 数量暴涨。
  • connector/system integration 种类更多。
  • permission model 更复杂。
  • data residency/regional requirements 更多。
  • noisy neighbor 风险上升。
  • support/debug burden 上升。
  • platform APIs 必须更 self-service。
  • deployment/rollback 需要更安全。
  • observability 必须按 customer impact 聚合。

面试回答：

At that scale, core infra should focus on standardization and guardrails. You can’t rely on tribal knowledge or one-off integrations. You need clear platform contracts, self-service tooling, tenant isolation, SLOs, automated rollout, and customer-impact observability.

27. Moveworks Deep Dive：Developer velocity framework#

这个岗位强调 frameworks 让 teams 快速 build customer-facing features。你可以讲 golden path：

  • new tool template。
  • schema and permission declaration。
  • local simulator。
  • replay test from production trace。
  • standard retry/timeout。
  • observability auto-instrumentation。
  • audit auto-logging。
  • approval hook。
  • canary rollout。
  • feature flag。

目标：

product engineer 不需要重复实现 auth/retry/audit。 · platform 自动 enforce safety。 · 新 workflow 上线更快，但不牺牲 reliability。

Standout line：

The best infra framework makes the safe, observable, scalable path the default path.

text

30. Red flags you can avoid better than other candidates#

把 data systems 只讲成 Airflow ETL。 · 不提 training run reproducibility。 · 不提 data contamination。 · 不提 dataloader/GPU input bottleneck。 · 不提 researcher tooling。 · 不提 dataset version diff。

Moveworks red flags：

把 agent 讲成 prompt + tool call。 · 不提 permission/policy。 · 不提 side effects/idempotency。 · 不提 audit。 · 不提 multi-tenant noisy neighbor。 · 不提 product teams 如何 safely extend platform。

31. Strong closing statement for each role#

xAI closing：

What excites me is building the data layer that compounds model progress. If researchers can find, build, validate, version, and consume training data faster, while preserving lineage and reproducibility, that directly accelerates model iteration.

Moveworks closing：

What excites me is building the core infrastructure that makes agentic AI dependable for enterprises. The platform has to let teams ship workflows quickly, but with policy, durable execution, observability, audit, and resilience built in by default.

32. Interview answer pattern：Depth without rambling#

用 5 层回答法：

  1. Principle

先给一句判断。

  1. Architecture

讲主要 components。

  1. Failure modes

讲哪里会坏。

  1. Metrics

讲怎么衡量。

  1. Tradeoff

讲为什么不是唯一答案。

Example for xAI：

Principle: dataset publication should be treated like deployment. Architecture: recipe registry, processing DAG, manifest store, lineage, quality gates. Failure modes: partial publish, contamination, schema drift, dedup bug. Metrics: build latency, sample rejection, dataloader throughput, eval impact. Tradeoff: fast experimentation vs production reproducibility.

Example for Moveworks：

Principle: agent reasoning should be separated from execution authority. Architecture: planner, tool registry, policy engine, durable executor, audit log. Failure modes: invalid tool call, permission miss, side-effect retry, downstream outage. Metrics: task success, tool latency, policy block, escalation, SLO. Tradeoff: developer velocity vs platform guardrails.

Knowledge Base / Enterprise Knowledge Systems Interview Prep#

这一节补 knowledge base 相关准备。它和 multimodal、AI infra、agentic AI 都强相关，尤其适合 Moveworks-style enterprise AI、Adobe-style enterprise content workflow、internal AI assistant、RAG platform、support automation、training data systems 面试。

核心定位：

A knowledge base is not just a vector database. It is a governed, versioned, permission-aware, freshness-aware, searchable knowledge layer that agents and RAG systems can trust.

中文理解：

Knowledge base 的难点不在 “把文档 embed 进 vector DB”，而在于文档从哪里来、谁能看、是否新鲜、如何 chunk、如何检索、如何引用、如何评估、如何同步、如何处理冲突、如何让 agent 安全使用。

1. Knowledge base 和 RAG 有什么关系？#

RAG 是 retrieval-augmented generation；knowledge base 是 retrieval 的可信数据基础。RAG 的质量上限往往由 knowledge base 决定：

文档是否完整。 · chunk 是否合理。 · metadata 是否丰富。 · ACL 是否正确。 · index 是否新鲜。 · retrieval 是否召回正确证据。 · citations 是否可验证。 · stale/conflicting knowledge 是否处理。

面试金句：

RAG quality is usually a knowledge systems problem before it is a prompt engineering problem.

2. Enterprise knowledge base 的核心 requirements#

Functional：

ingest docs from many sources。 · search/retrieve relevant knowledge。 · answer with citations。 · support ACL/permissions。 · support freshness/update/delete。 · support structured and unstructured data。 · support feedback and eval。

Non-functional：

  • permission correctness。
  • low latency。
  • high freshness。
  • high recall。
  • explainability。
  • auditability。
  • multi-tenant isolation。
  • cost control。
  • operational visibility。

Enterprise-specific：

  • documents live in many systems：Google Drive、SharePoint、Confluence、Slack、Jira、ServiceNow、GitHub、CRM、DAM、CMS。
  • permissions differ by source。
  • data changes constantly。
  • users expect answers to respect their access。
  • stale answer can be worse than no answer。

3. Knowledge base architecture#

典型 architecture：

Connectors · pull/push data from source systems。

Ingestion Pipeline · fetch、parse、normalize、dedup、classify、extract metadata。

Permission Sync · document ACL、group membership、user mapping、tenant boundary。

Chunking Service · semantic chunk、layout chunk、table chunk、code chunk、conversation chunk。

Embedding Service · text/image/document embeddings，versioned embedding model。

Index Layer · BM25 / vector index / hybrid index / graph index。

Metadata Store · source、owner、timestamp、version、permissions、document type、quality。

Retrieval API · query understanding、ACL filtering、hybrid retrieval、reranking。

Answer Service · LLM/VLM answer with citations。

Feedback/Eval · thumbs up/down、citation correctness、retrieval recall、answer faithfulness。

Admin/Debug UI · ingestion status、permission debugging、index freshness、query trace。

4. Ingestion pipeline 怎么设计？#

Pipeline：

  1. connector fetches document。
  2. source version and checksum recorded。
  3. parser extracts text/media/layout。
  4. metadata and ACL extracted。
  5. content normalized。
  6. chunks generated。
  7. chunks embedded。
  8. indexes updated。
  9. lineage recorded。
  10. quality checks and freshness metrics updated。

关键设计：

Raw source snapshot should be immutable。 · Parsed text and chunks should be versioned。 · Embedding model version must be recorded。 · Index update should be atomic or transactional enough to avoid partial document state。 · Delete/update from source must propagate。

Failure modes：

connector silently fails。 · permissions drift。 · parser drops tables/images。 · chunking breaks semantic meaning。 · stale chunks remain after doc deletion。 · embedding version mismatch。 · index partially updated。

5. Chunking 怎么做才好？#

Chunking 是 RAG 的核心之一。不要只说 fixed-size chunks。

Chunking strategies：

Fixed-size chunking · simple, but may split concepts。

Semantic chunking · split by headings/paragraph/topic。

Layout-aware chunking · for PDF/slides/docs，preserve page、section、table、figure。

Hierarchical chunking · document -> section -> paragraph；retrieval can return parent context。

Conversation chunking · preserve thread, speaker, timestamp。

Code chunking · function/class/module aware。

Table chunking · keep header, row groups, schema。

Good chunk metadata：

  • doc_id。
  • chunk_id。
  • source。
  • title。
  • section path。
  • page number。
  • bbox。
  • timestamp。
  • author/owner。
  • created/updated time。
  • ACL。
  • embedding version。

Tradeoff：

小 chunk recall 精准，但 context 不够。 · 大 chunk context 完整，但 retrieval 噪音大。 · 最好支持 parent-child retrieval：先找 small chunks，再带 parent context。

6. ACL / permission filtering 为什么是硬要求？#

Enterprise RAG 最大红线是 permission leak。用户不能因为 AI assistant 而看到自己本来无权访问的文档。

原则：

ACL filtering before generation · LLM 不应看到无权限 chunks。

Prefer pre-retrieval filtering when possible · 在 retrieval 前或 retrieval 中过滤，而不是 answer 后再删。

Permission sync must be fresh · group membership、document sharing、employee status 都会变。

Citations must also obey ACL · 不能引用用户打不开的 source。

Implementation patterns：

store ACL metadata per document/chunk。 · maintain user/group mapping。 · pushdown filters into search index。 · cache permission carefully with TTL。 · audit access decisions。 · test with synthetic users。

面试金句：

In enterprise RAG, permission correctness is more important than answer completeness.

7. Hybrid retrieval 怎么设计？#

单纯 vector search 不够。推荐 hybrid：

BM25 / lexical search · good for exact terms、IDs、error codes、names。

Vector search · good for semantic meaning。

Metadata filtering · source、time、owner、type、tenant、permissions。

Graph traversal · related docs、tickets、services、owners。

Reranking · cross-encoder / LLM reranker / domain-specific ranker。

Retrieval workflow：

  1. query understanding。
  2. rewrite or expand query。
  3. apply ACL and metadata filters。
  4. retrieve from lexical + vector。
  5. merge and dedup。
  6. rerank。
  7. pack context。
  8. answer with citations。

Metrics：

recall@K。 · MRR/NDCG。 · citation hit rate。 · answer faithfulness。 · latency。 · no-answer rate。

8. Knowledge freshness 怎么保证？#

Freshness 是 enterprise KB 难点。Stale answer 会造成事故。

Strategies：

event-driven updates from source webhooks。 · periodic full crawl。 · incremental sync with cursor。 · document version checksum。 · freshness SLA by source。 · deletion tombstone。 · stale badge in answer。 · retrieval boost for fresh docs。

Freshness metrics：

source update to index availability latency。 · connector lag。 · stale document rate。 · failed sync rate。 · deleted-but-searchable count。

面试回答：

I would treat freshness as an SLO. For high-value sources like IT tickets or policy docs, the system should expose connector lag and freshness dashboards, not just search latency.

9. Knowledge conflicts 怎么处理？#

Enterprise knowledge often conflicts：

old policy vs new policy。 · regional variations。 · draft vs approved。 · team-specific runbook。 · duplicated docs。 · Slack answer contradicts official docs。

Resolution strategies：

source authority ranking。 · freshness ranking。 · approval status。 · owner/team metadata。 · region/tenant filters。 · answer should mention conflict when unresolved。 · route to human owner if high-risk。

Answer pattern：

I found conflicting sources. The approved HR policy updated on May 10 says X, while an older team note says Y. I would cite the approved policy and flag the older note as stale.

10. Knowledge graph 和 vector index 怎么结合？#

Vector index 找语义相似；knowledge graph 表达实体关系。

KG useful for：

employee -> team -> permissions。 · service -> owner -> runbook -> incidents。 · product -> feature -> docs -> tickets。 · asset -> campaign -> performance。 · document -> version -> owner。

Hybrid design：

vector retrieval finds candidate chunks。 · entity extraction links chunks to graph entities。 · graph expands related context。 · reranker scores final evidence。

Example：

用户问 “Why is checkout failing in EU?” Vector search 找到 incident notes；KG 找 service owner、EU deployment、recent config change、runbook。

11. Multimodal knowledge base 怎么设计？#

Multimodal KB 不只存 text，还存：

  • PDF page images。
  • slides。
  • screenshots。
  • diagrams。
  • tables。
  • charts。
  • video transcripts。
  • keyframes。
  • audio transcripts。
  • design assets。

Architecture extensions：

OCR/layout extraction。 · image embeddings。 · chart/table parser。 · video scene index。 · ASR transcript with timestamps。 · visual citations：page/bbox/frame/timestamp。

回答重点：

A multimodal knowledge base should preserve evidence structure. Converting every image, table, and video into plain text loses grounding.

12. Agent 如何使用 knowledge base？#

Agent 使用 KB 不只是问答，还会做 workflow：

retrieve policy before action。 · inspect runbook before executing tool。 · summarize related tickets。 · find owner。 · draft response。 · create ticket or workflow。

Agent-KB safety：

retrieved content is context, not instruction。 · tool execution still needs policy。 · citations required for factual claims。 · high-risk actions need approval。 · stale/conflicting knowledge should block or escalate。

Moveworks-style answer：

For enterprise agents, the knowledge base is the grounding layer, but the policy engine is still the authority layer.

13. Knowledge base observability#

需要观察：

  • connector health。
  • ingestion lag。
  • parse failure。
  • chunk count changes。
  • embedding queue latency。
  • index freshness。
  • permission sync lag。
  • query latency。
  • retrieval recall。
  • no-result rate。
  • answer citation correctness。
  • user feedback by source。

Debug query trace should show：

original query。 · rewritten query。 · filters applied。 · retrieved chunks。 · ACL decisions。 · rerank scores。 · final citations。 · answer generation prompt/context。

14. Knowledge base eval 怎么做？#

Eval categories：

retrieval eval：query -> expected docs/chunks。 · answer eval：answer correctness。 · citation eval：citation supports answer。 · permission eval：unauthorized user cannot retrieve。 · freshness eval：updated doc appears quickly。 · conflict eval：model handles conflicting docs。 · no-answer eval：model refuses when KB lacks evidence。

Gold set construction：

real user queries。 · synthetic queries from docs。 · adversarial permission tests。 · stale/conflict test cases。 · high-risk policy questions。

面试金句：

I would evaluate the knowledge base as a retrieval system, an answer system, and a permission system separately.

15. System design：Enterprise Knowledge Base for Agentic AI#

Requirements：

many enterprise sources。 · permission-aware search。 · citations。 · agent tool grounding。 · freshness。 · multi-tenant。 · admin/debug UI。

Architecture：

  • connectors。
  • source snapshot store。
  • parser/chunker。
  • ACL sync service。
  • embedding/index service。
  • metadata catalog。
  • retrieval/rerank API。
  • answer service。
  • agent context API。
  • eval/feedback。
  • admin console。

State model：

text
document(
  doc_id,
  tenant_id,
  source_type,
  source_uri,
  version,
  checksum,
  acl_ref,
  updated_at,
  indexed_at,
  status
)

chunk(
  chunk_id,
  doc_id,
  text_ref,
  embedding_ref,
  page,
  bbox,
  section_path,
  metadata,
  embedding_model_version
)

query_trace(
  query_id,
  user_id,
  tenant_id,
  query,
  filters,
  retrieved_chunk_ids,
  denied_chunk_count,
  answer_id,
  feedback
)

16. Project Story：Permission-aware Enterprise Knowledge Platform#

一句话：

I built a permission-aware enterprise knowledge platform that ingests documents from multiple systems, preserves ACLs and freshness, supports hybrid retrieval, and gives agents grounded citations.

Problem：

enterprise knowledge scattered across docs, tickets, chat, PDFs, videos。 · search was stale and permission handling inconsistent。 · AI answers lacked citations and sometimes used old docs。

Architecture：

connectors + ingestion pipeline。 · ACL sync。 · semantic/layout chunking。 · hybrid lexical/vector index。 · reranker。 · citation-aware answer service。 · query trace UI。 · eval suite。

Pain points：

permissions changed faster than content。 · old docs outranked new docs。 · tables/screenshots lost meaning after text extraction。 · users asked questions that require multiple sources。

Impact metrics：

answer citation support rate。 · permission leak tests pass。 · freshness SLA。 · no-answer quality。 · support deflection。 · time to find policy/runbook。

17. Project Story：Knowledge Debugging Console#

一句话：

I built an internal knowledge debugging console that lets engineers and support teams inspect why an AI answer retrieved certain documents, whether ACL filters applied, and where freshness or parsing failed.

Why it stands out：

很多候选人只讲 RAG pipeline，不讲 debug tooling。 · Enterprise AI 的可运营性依赖 debug console。

Features：

query replay。 · retrieved chunks and scores。 · denied chunks due to ACL。 · source freshness。 · parse/chunk preview。 · citation validation。 · feedback annotation。 · escalation to doc owner。

Metrics：

time to debug bad answer。 · unsupported answer rate。 · stale source incidents。 · query eval coverage。

18. Knowledge base 面试高频追问#

1. 为什么不用纯 vector DB？#

因为 enterprise queries 常需要 exact match、IDs、permissions、freshness、source authority、metadata filters。Vector DB 只是 index layer，不是 knowledge system。

2. ACL filtering 放在哪里？#

尽量 retrieval 前或 retrieval 中 pushdown。至少 generation 前必须过滤。绝不能让 LLM 看到 unauthorized content 后再指望它不说。

3. 如何处理 stale docs？#

source version、freshness SLA、connector lag dashboard、updated_at ranking、stale warning、approved source priority。

4. 如何处理 no answer？#

如果 KB 没证据，应该 say no answer / ask clarification / escalate，不应编造。No-answer quality 是重要 eval。

5. 如何让 agent 不被 KB 内容 prompt injection？#

把 retrieved content 标记为 untrusted evidence；system prompt 明确 KB content cannot override instructions；tool execution 走 policy engine。

6. 如何降低 RAG latency？#

cache embeddings、hybrid retrieval parallel、precompute ACL filters、rerank top N not top all、optimize chunk count、use source filters、context budget control。

19. Knowledge base standout one-liners#

A vector database is not a knowledge base. · RAG quality starts with knowledge quality. · Permission correctness is more important than answer completeness. · Freshness should be an SLO, not a best effort. · Citations should point to evidence, not just documents. · Retrieved content is context, not authority. · Knowledge debugging tooling is part of the product, not an internal luxury. · Evaluate retrieval, generation, and permissions separately.

20. How to connect KB to xAI and Moveworks#

xAI angle：

training data catalog is a kind of knowledge base for model teams。 · dataset lineage resembles document lineage。 · internal tooling helps researchers find, inspect, and trust data。 · eval failures can be debugged through data slices and metadata。

Moveworks angle：

enterprise agent needs permission-aware KB。 · support automation depends on fresh policies/runbooks/tickets。 · tool execution should be grounded in retrieved knowledge but authorized by policy。 · core infra can provide shared retrieval/context APIs for product teams。

Adobe angle：

  • creative asset knowledge base includes images, videos, templates, brand kits, campaign performance。
  • multimodal KB supports visual search, brand compliance, content provenance, and campaign generation。

Behavioral / Leadership 答法#

1. 如何回答 “你做过 multimodal 项目吗？”#

模板：

我做过/可以设计的核心不是单一模型调用，而是 end-to-end multimodal workflow。我会从 media ingestion 开始，处理 upload、decode、OCR/ASR、embedding、model inference、artifact storage、observability、evaluation、安全和成本。比如在 visual/document/video RAG 中，我会把 OCR、ASR、keyframes、visual embeddings、metadata 都作为 first-class evidence，并要求答案带 page/bbox/timestamp citation。

2. 如何展示你理解 product impact？#

不要只说 accuracy。说：

time to first preview。 · accepted asset rate。 · export/share rate。 · user trust。 · support ticket reduction。 · workflow completion。 · cost per accepted asset。 · safety incident rate。

3. 如何回答 “模型效果好但线上慢怎么办？”#

回答：

  • 先拆 latency：preprocess、queue、encoder、LLM、decode、postprocess。
  • 看是否可以 cache media features。
  • preview/final split。
  • model routing。
  • token compression。
  • async workflow。
  • batching。
  • specialized small models。
  • product-level fallback。

4. 如何回答 “你如何和 research/model team 合作？”#

我会把 model capability 转成 production contract：

input/output schema。 · media constraints。 · latency/cost profile。 · model version。 · eval report。 · safety behavior。 · failure modes。 · rollback plan。

Research 关注 capability，infra 关注 reliability/cost/security，product 关注 workflow。好的 tech lead 要把三者接起来。

Cheat Sheet#

面试必须会说的关键词#

CLIP, contrastive learning, dual encoder, cross encoder, ViT, visual tokens, projector, Q-Former, Perceiver Resampler, cross-attention, LLaVA-style, Flamingo-style, VQA, OCR, grounding, visual hallucination, multimodal RAG, video keyframes, ASR, spectrogram, diffusion, DiT, asset graph, content provenance, model routing, media pipeline, feature cache, evaluation platform。

一句话总结 Multimodal System#

Multimodal system = media ingestion + representation extraction + cross-modal alignment + reasoning/generation + grounding/evaluation + artifact workflow + safety/cost/observability。

一句话总结 Multimodal 的最大难点#

最大难点不是把图片塞进 LLM，而是让模型的输出 truly grounded in media evidence，并且在真实产品中可服务、可调试、可评估、可治理。

面试收尾表达#

My approach to multimodal AI is to treat media as first-class structured evidence and first-class production assets. The model matters, but the system around it - ingestion, alignment, retrieval, evaluation, safety, provenance, and workflow - determines whether the capability becomes a reliable product.
